prc-Text-Text-9mHv3" data-component="Text"><div>feat: open contact loci and uniform affine deformations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6234043747" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/issues/28976" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/28976/hovercard" href="https://github.com/TauCetiProject/TauCeti/pull/28976">#28976</a>)</div></span><span class="ws-pre-wrap extended-commit-description-container f6 wb-break-word text-mono mt-2 prc-Text-Text-9mHv3" data-component="Text">This PR proves that contactness is open for continuous covector/bilinear-form families on a finite-dimensional space, and that two such pairs agreeing on a subset have a contact affine interpolation on an open neighborhood, uniformly over any compact parameter set. Keep this as the complete coefficient-level prerequisite for contact deformation arguments; no manifold neighborhood or isotopy theorem is claimed.

Advance `SymplecticGeometry/ContactGeometry/README.md`, milestone CG3, “Darboux and Gray stability”: the contact Darboux proof must keep its local contact-Moser path contact while shrinking the spatial neighborhood over the compact time interval. The linear normal form and horizontal contact-Moser equation already exist on main. The explicit downstream consumer is CG5, “Isotropic and Legendrian neighborhoods”, which requires that forms agreeing in full value and exterior derivative along the zero section have contact affine interpolation on a neighborhood for every t ∈ [0,1]. In a local tangent-bundle trivialization, apply `exists_isOpen_isContact_affine` to the covector values and exterior-derivative bilinear forms, with K = [0,1]. After this PR, the manifold/trivialization adapter and integration of the resulting time-dependent contact-Moser field remain before the contact Darboux/Gray and Legendrian neighborhood theorems can be concluded.

Roadmap: SymplecticGeometry

&lt;!--tauceti-target:v1 {"focus":"SymplecticGeometry","id":"contact-affine-homotopy-neighborhood"}--&gt;

Add `TauCeti/Topology/Algebra/Module/Contact.lean` (98 lines), with two public theorems and no new definitions. The first theorem uses the existing `Module.Dual.isContact_iff_nondegenerate_symplectizationForm` and Mathlib's determinant criterion for nondegeneracy. The second uses Mathlib's `IsCompact.eventually_forall_of_forall_eventually`; compactness is needed only for the parameter set, not the agreement locus. No topology or norm on the vector space, alternation assumption on the bilinear forms, or closedness of the agreement locus is imposed. The field needs continuous ring operations and closed singletons, not continuity of inversion.

References credited in the module: Geiges, *An Introduction to Contact Topology*, §2.5, and Kriegl–Michor, *The Convenient Setting of Global Analysis*, Proposition 43.18. No Mathlib infrastructure or external formalization is vendored.

Read the shared protocol and all ten rubrics: scope, correctness, reuse, attribution, api-design, generality, placement, naming, documentation, and proof-quality. Self-review against API design, reuse and generality found no existing replacement; retain only the complete algebraic/topological prerequisite, remove the unfinished form adapter, and simplify the final proof step. The scope is deliberately smaller than the full geometric neighborhood result.

Local checks, not CI: `lake exe cache get`, plain whole-library `lake build --iofail` (67,514 jobs), changed-module axiom audit, all 14 environment linters, and whitespace checking pass. CI still verifies the pushed head, including transitive axioms and global ownership.
